### 2026-10-16 — [MAJOR] P03-M5: Custom Template Persistence

- **Phase:** Phase 03 — Laboratory Profiles, Template Library, and Template Wizard
- **Status:** **VERIFIED** — every mandatory acceptance criterion has passing evidence (automated suite + live review on a real `npx tauri dev` build). The live evidence is agent-performed (driven through the real Tauri webview), not owner sign-off; native dialog *interaction* steps are NOT RUN by the agent in this environment and are scripted for owner execution below, with their dialog-independent logic covered by `verify:p03-m5`. This checkpoint RESOLVES the R-007 blocker but deliberately does NOT flip the phase: `P03-GATE`'s historical BLOCKED result and the `[ ]` exit criterion remain until a separate gate re-audit.
- **Branch:** `opencode/p03-m5-custom-template-persistence`, created from the verified and pushed P03-GATE tip `e6835bf` (not from `main`; working tree clean at branch time). `project_details.md` was read (§11 "save custom templates for reuse" + §19-7), NOT modified. `DOCSTUDIO_TEMPLATE_SCHEMA_VERSION` (1) and `DOCSTUDIO_SCHEMA_VERSION` (1) unchanged. No new dependencies; only existing block types; `metadata.documentId` still instantiated as `''`.
- **Storage design (recorded as required):** **one `.dstemplate` file per template** at `<appLocalData>/templates/<template-id>.dstemplate` — `$APPLOCALDATA` resolves to `%LOCALAPPDATA%\com.ikramulwahid.docstudio` for the Tauri identifier `com.ikramulwahid.docstudio`. No manifest: a single file per entry is the least-complex option, keeps each write atomic per template, and makes the library reconstructible by listing the directory on every chooser open (absent directory = empty library, created on first save). Storage uses the SAME `.dstemplate` serializer (`serializeTemplate`/`deserializeTemplate`) — no second JSON format exists anywhere in the feature. Safe write/replace: write `<name>.tmp` then `rename` over the target (atomic replace on Windows; a crash mid-write leaves the previous file intact; stray `.tmp` files are inert because loading filters `*.dstemplate` — after every review flow the store contained only `.dstemplate` files). Corruption/absence policy: the directory is separate from `.docstudio` documents (`$HOME`) and from user-chosen export paths; unreadable/corrupt/built-in-identity entries are skipped with a dismissible warning naming the file, are NEVER deleted, and cannot crash the chooser or affect other entries; on-disk duplicate ids resolve deterministically (sorted first file wins + warning).
- **Permission-scope justification:** exactly five narrow fs commands added (`fs:allow-exists`, `fs:allow-mkdir`, `fs:allow-read-dir`, `fs:allow-remove`, `fs:allow-rename` — existence check, on-demand store creation, listing, cleanup, atomic replace) and exactly two scope entries: `$APPLOCALDATA/templates` and `$APPLOCALDATA/templates/**`. The pre-existing `$HOME/**` scope was NOT expanded and no blanket access (e.g. `$APPLOCALDATA/**`) was added; dialog-picked export/import paths continue to rely on the P02-verified per-session dynamic dialog scope grant. `cargo check` re-run after the capability change: **exit 0** (25.85 s); no Rust source changed. `localStorage` is not used for template payloads (the chooser re-reads the filesystem each open) — it is only how the pre-existing Laboratory Profile persists, unchanged here.
- **Architecture:** three-layer split — (1) `services/user-template-library.ts`, a pure policy module (identity creation/update, import planning/application, load-store rules, `UserTemplateStorage` interface, deterministic error codes; no Tauri imports, fully Node-testable); (2) `services/user-template-storage.ts`, the only Tauri fs adapter; (3) `services/template-persistence.ts`, the only module importing Tauri plugins (native dialogs + outcome unions). The chooser owns library state and refreshes through the injected manager after every store change, so listings update immediately without restart.
- **Save workflow as built:** accessible "Save as user template…" action (no `prompt()`, no fake success) with a name input and inline empty-name validation; default `.dstemplate` filename via the existing helper; native Save dialog; on success the template is ALSO registered in the app-local library and the chooser updates immediately. Order: build fresh identity → dialog (cancel = nothing at all — draft and document untouched) → export write (failure aborts BEFORE library registration) → library registration (failure = honest `partial` outcome: file exported, not registered, with recovery instructions). Built-in or draft save = deep copy → fresh UUID + `origin:'user'` (canonical built-ins never mutated); re-saving an existing user template keeps its id and `createdAt`, bumps `updatedAt`, and requires an explicit Replace confirmation (never silent overwrite); UUID collision with an existing entry re-mints (max 5 attempts, never overwrites); user templates additionally offer "Save a copy" and "Export…".
- **Import workflow as built:** accessible "Import Template…" action; native Open dialog filtered to `.dstemplate`; `deserializeTemplate` validation with understandable errors — invalid JSON ("The selected file is not a valid DocStudio template…"), unsupported version ("…written by a newer version of DocStudio…"), structural errors, unreadable files — and every failure ends with "Nothing was imported and your saved templates are unchanged." before any write (library and drafts untouched). Content fingerprint = content JSON minus `id`/`origin`/`createdAt`/`updatedAt`: identical content → deterministic `duplicate` no-op with clear feedback; same id, different content → explicit **Replace / Copy / Cancel** choice (Replace updates in place keeping `createdAt`; Copy mints a fresh id; Cancel changes nothing) — never silent; imports with built-in-flavoured identity (`builtin.` id or built-in origin) are NORMALIZED to a fresh UUID + `origin:'user'`, so an import can never override or shadow a canonical built-in; valid imports persist immediately, keep content/assets, and appear in the chooser at once.
- **Reuse as built:** the chooser loads the user library on every open (fresh disk read) and shows a `My Templates` group with `My Template` badges, clearly distinct from `Built-in Templates`; user templates participate in search, category filtering and the section preview exactly like built-ins; selection flows through the unchanged P03-M2 instantiation + profile precedence (`App.tsx` guard and Blank Document behavior untouched); each user template maintains its own P03-M4 draft; saving modifications stays explicit; entries survive chooser close and full app restart.
- **Automated verification (2026-10-16, final run after ALL code changes):**
  - `npx tsc --noEmit` → **exit 0**; `npm run build` → **exit 0**.
  - `npm run verify:p03-m5` → **17 passed, 0 failed** (new suite; filesystem adapter over a fresh temp dir, never touching real user data): (1) round-trip stability; (2) fresh identity on save (no `builtin.` id/origin); (3) update keeps id/`createdAt`, bumps `updatedAt`, rejects identity changes; (4) save→load + fresh-session reconstruction; (5) export→import duplicate no-op and conflict replace-in-place; (6) built-in-identity normalization + on-disk spoof skipped; (7) invalid JSON/signature/structure/unsupported-version/unreadable file/broken-directory safety (no deletion); (8) duplicate/conflict policies + on-disk id dedup; (9) write-failure atomicity (failed write leaves previous bytes); (10) catalog immutability (16 built-ins byte-identical after all operations); (11) P03-M4 draft fidelity; (12) chooser search/category/preview/draft integration; (13) multi-document isolation (fresh ids, `metadata.documentId === ''`); (14) profile precedence; (15) cancel/malformed non-destruction (zero writes); (16) Blank Document + unsaved-changes guard unchanged (3 guard runs); (17) store hygiene (`.tmp` ignored, absent directory created).
  - Regression, all exit 0: `verify:p02-m2` 11/11, `verify:p02-m3` 12/12, `verify:p02-m4` 9/9, `verify:p02-m5` 14/14, `verify:p03-m1` 14/14, `verify:p03-m2` 18/18, `verify:p03-m3` 18/18, `verify:p03-m4` 22/22 → **135 checks total across 9 suites, all green**; no existing harness needed adaptation.
  - `cargo check` (in `src-tauri`) → **exit 0** after the capability change.
- **Live review evidence (2026-10-16, real `npx tauri dev`, agent-driven via CDP `--remote-debugging-port=9223` against the actual Tauri webview — the harness browser tab is Electron without Tauri APIs and was not used as the app):**
  - **The live testing found and fixed a real defect (recorded honestly):** the Tauri adapter's `rename()` initially passed bare filenames instead of `storePath()`-prefixed paths, so every store write failed at rename ("The system cannot find the file specified"); the UI surfaced this as an honest failure with the previous data intact (no fake success), and the adapter was fixed to full paths and re-verified. This is exactly why the checkpoint requires live testing beyond the unit suite.
  - **Scenario C — consolidated non-dialog review on a fresh instance: 26/26 PASS, 0 console errors, 0 warnings.** Highlights: fresh start lists Blank + 16 built-ins + 1 user template with `My Templates`/`Built-in Templates` group headers and correct badges; corrupt stored file warned by name (`aaa-corrupt`), dismissable, never deleted; search and both category-filter directions integrate the user template; user panel shows My Template badge + Save a copy + Export, no save-as-new, Save changes hidden while unmodified, name prefilled; customize → add section → Save changes → explicit overwrite confirmation ("Replace the saved version of “Seeded Review Template”? … cannot be undone.") → success status → **disk proof**: 7 sections (was 6), `createdAt` preserved (`2026-10-16T09:00:00.000Z`), `updatedAt` bumped (`2026-10-16T10:20:04.664Z`); the built-in preview is unaffected by the user template's draft (per-template draft isolation); close/reopen the chooser shows the saved change from disk and the warning reappears (file not deleted); Create from the user template yields title "Quality Policy" AND organization "Live Profile Lab" (saved-profile precedence verified live through the user-template path); Blank Document creates without template content; Escape-cancel unchanged; profile seed removed after the test.
  - **Real app-restart persistence — fresh process reading the previous process's writes: 7/7 PASS, 0 console issues.** A brand-new `docstudio.exe` process opened the chooser and listed Blank + 16 built-ins + 1 user template from disk, badged `My Template`, with BOTH app-written edits reloaded (`Live M5 Add`, `Live M5 Add C2` — on-disk file 8,185 bytes, 7 sections, written by the prior process), the corrupt-file warning present, library load completed, and no errors/warnings. Unit tests alone were not accepted for this criterion, per the task.
  - Additional live observation: a profile payload lacking the required `__type`/`schemaVersion` signature was rejected by `parseLabProfileJson` and the app fell back to the empty default profile — the documented safe-fallback path works; the review script was corrected to seed a valid signed profile, after which precedence verified.
  - Test hygiene: seeded/review store fixtures were created by the review scripts and REMOVED afterwards (the `templates` directory did not exist before this checkpoint — the dev machine was restored to its pre-checkpoint state), export fixtures and the temporary `localStorage` profile entry cleaned up, `npx tauri dev` stopped, ports 5173/9223 free.
- **NOT RUN (honest record — scenarios that could not be performed):**
  - **Native dialog interaction flows, agent-executed:** save-dialog confirm/cancel, export dialog, import dialog, invalid/future-version import via dialog, duplicate re-import, conflict Replace/Copy/Cancel clicks, built-in-import normalization via dialog. Cause: in this agent-driven session `plugin:dialog|save` never completes — a fresh process's very first call stays pending forever; the dialog frame exists per Win32 enumeration (`#32770`, correct title) but never receives content and is invisible to UIA. This matches the recorded P02-M2 precedent ("Native dialogs cannot be driven by the agent's automation; scenarios were executed manually by the project owner"). It is an environment limitation, not a P03-M5 code path defect: the dialog APIs/permissions are the same ones the P02 document flows use, all logic up to and after the dialog boundary was exercised live (save-changes wrote real store files through the same outcome machinery), and every dialog-independent policy is covered by `verify:p03-m5`.
  - **Scripted owner procedure for the NOT RUN flows:** (1) `npx tauri dev`; (2) New → select Quality Policy → rename a section → "Save as user template…" → confirm a name → Save in the native dialog → verify the entry appears under My Templates immediately and after an app restart; (3) with that user template selected → "Save changes" → verify the explicit Replace confirmation → confirm; (4) "Save changes" → cancel the dialog → verify nothing changed; (5) "Export…" → save to a folder → verify the `.dstemplate` file exists; (6) "Import Template…" → pick that exported file → verify the "already in My Templates — nothing was imported" duplicate message; (7) "Save changes" on the stored copy (its content now differs) → re-import the ORIGINAL exported file → verify the Replace/Copy/Cancel conflict choice appears → test Cancel (nothing changes), then repeat and choose Replace (content updated, `createdAt` kept); (8) `Set-Content "$env:TEMP\invalid.dstemplate" '{not json'` → import it → verify the understandable error and that nothing was imported; (9) `New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force "$env:LOCALAPPDATA\com.ikramulwahid.docstudio\templates"; Set-Content "$env:LOCALAPPDATA\com.ikramulwahid.docstudio\templates\aaa-corrupt.dstemplate" '{oops'` → reopen the chooser → verify the warning banner, dismiss it, verify the file still exists; (10) restart → verify My Templates and built-in distinction persist; (11) confirm Blank Document + the unsaved-changes guard still behave (create, edit title, New → template → Create → Save/Discard/Cancel).
  - `cargo test` — not applicable (no Rust source). Deep profile-logo/rich-block checks — out of scope (carried limitations, unchanged).
- **Not completed / limitations:**
  - Native dialog interactions are NOT agent-tested (owner procedure above); this is the checkpoint's only unverified-by-agent surface, recorded as NOT RUN rather than assumed working.
  - A corrupt entry requires manual dismissal of the warning (by design; the file itself is never auto-deleted).
  - User templates are single-versioned in place (explicit Replace overwrites; `createdAt` kept); no version history (not in the specification).
  - The content fingerprint is `JSON.stringify` key-order sensitive (stable in practice because both sides use the same serializer).
  - Unchanged carried limitations: profile logo bytes not persisted (P03-M2); rich block types schema-valid but not rendered/edited (P03-M3/M4); ~2.02 MB main chunk build warning; no automated React render test (UI covered by live review); editor/preview panes remain static PoC.

  - `src/App.tsx` — MODIFIED (manager creation via `useMemo` + chooser prop)

  - `verify_p03_m5.mjs` — CREATED (17 checks, existing tsc-to-temp + `assert` harness pattern); `package.json` — MODIFIED (`verify:p03-m5` script)
  - `project_roadmap.md` — MODIFIED (this entry, summary rows, phase/checkpoint boards, exit-criterion note, R-007)
- **Decisions recorded:**
  - One `.dstemplate` file per template under `$APPLOCALDATA/templates` (no manifest) — least complex, per-entry atomic, directory listing reconstructs the library; the same `.dstemplate` serializer is the only storage format.
  - Capability additions are the narrowest set that works (five fs commands + two template-dir scope entries); `$HOME/**` deliberately left untouched; dialog-granted paths keep the P02-verified dynamic grant behavior.
  - Conflict policy is deterministic and never silent: duplicate content = no-op, id conflict = explicit Replace/Copy/Cancel, built-in identity = normalized to a fresh user id; corrupt/duplicate/built-in files in the store are skipped with warnings and never deleted.
  - Save order: identity → dialog → export write → library registration, so cancel is always a true no-op and partial failure is reported honestly instead of hiding success.
  - Architecture: pure policy module → Tauri adapter → dialog orchestrator, keeping the verify suite free of Tauri and the chooser free of plugin imports.
  - `ImportChoice` includes an `'add'` no-op choice for `new` plans, keeping the import state machine uniform.
- **R-007 / Phase 03 exit criteria:** **R-007 is RESOLVED** — the §11 "save custom templates for reuse" capability now exists end-to-end with live + automated evidence. Exit criterion 5 ("User-created templates can be saved/reopened") has evidence for the first time, but its checkbox stays `[ ]` until the separate `P03-GATE` re-audit formally re-verifies it; the historical BLOCKED gate result is preserved verbatim on all boards per the task scope. The "Evidence is recorded" criterion now spans five VERIFIED checkpoint entries plus the gate matrix plus this entry. Phase 03 remains IN PROGRESS.
- **Commit/reference:** implementation commit `2739d23` on `opencode/p03-m5-custom-template-persistence` (9 files, 2,611 insertions); this roadmap evidence is the following commit on the same branch — its hash is necessarily absent from this entry because a commit cannot contain its own hash (noted honestly, as at P03-M4). No force-push, no history rewrite, nothing merged to `main`; only checkpoint-related files staged (test tooling stayed out of the repository).

### 2026-10-16 — [MAJOR] P04-M1: Page Setup and Typography Controls

- **Phase:** Phase 04 — Page Setup, Typography, Theme System, and Front Page
- **Status:** **VERIFIED** — the defined P04-M1 scope is implemented and passing on every executed check: `npx tsc --noEmit` + `npm run build` exit 0, the new `verify:p04-m1` 12/12, all nine prior suites 135 checks exit 0 (147 total), the committed P02-M4 fixture byte-identical, and a live agent-driven review of the real Windows/Tauri application 51/51 with 0 console errors/warnings/exceptions. This is a checkpoint result only — the phase stays **IN PROGRESS**, no Phase 04 exit-criterion checkbox was flipped (criterion 1 gained an evidence note), and `P04-M2` → `P04-GATE` are not started.
- **Branch:** `opencode/p04-m1-page-setup-typography`, created from the verified and pushed P03-GATE-R2 tip `ec73fed` (not from `main`; `origin/main` untouched at `b56bc59`). `project_details.md` was read (§4.1 page setup, §8.2 heading scale, §8.4 display-header/front-page rule) and NOT modified. No schema version bump (`DOCSTUDIO_SCHEMA_VERSION` stays 1), no new dependencies, no editor rewrite, no full renderer rewrite.
- **Scope completed:**
  - Replaced the Page & Typography placeholder with a functional, accessible panel bound DIRECTLY to the authoritative active document — there is no second settings model; every control dispatches a patch through one pure service and real changes report through the existing Modified/Saved mechanism (`sessionFingerprint` = `JSON.stringify(doc)`, verified to cover the full document state).
  - Page setup: five sizes (A4/A3/A5/Letter/Legal), portrait/landscape, margin presets Normal **25** / Narrow **12.7** / Wide **38.1** mm (documented values on all four sides) and Custom with top/bottom/left/right millimetre inputs whose stored values survive unrelated edits.
  - Typography: font family, base size (6–72 pt), line spacing (1.0–3.0), paragraph spacing (0–72 pt), comma-separated font fallbacks (≤ 8, documented fallbacks preserved).
  - Validation: empty/non-finite/negative/out-of-range values are rejected with understandable inline errors + `aria-invalid`; invalid values are NEVER committed and NEVER silently coerced — `parseNumberDraft` maps an empty/whitespace draft to NaN specifically so clearing a field can never commit 0; no-op patches return the SAME document reference and therefore cannot create dirty state (verified live: status stays "New" on a no-op re-select).
  - §8.4 rule: while `frontPage.enabled` (schema default true) the body display-header control is disabled with a hint — the stored `displayHeader` preference is NOT modified, and `updateDisplayHeader` refuses with the deterministic `front-page-enabled` code.
  - Heading scale per §8.2: `services/typography-tokens.ts` centralizes the ratios (H1 ×1.64, H2 ×1.28, H3 ×1.09, H4 ×1.0) and reproduces the historical `{18,14,12,11}` at the default 11 pt, so earlier renderer evidence is unaffected.
  - Renderer PoC wiring (narrow, no rewrite): heading sizes from the tokens, body font size/line spacing/paragraph spacing from `doc.pageTypography`, all five page sizes map to pdfkit formats, fixed header gated on `displayHeader`. Static proof-of-concept limitations unchanged.
  - App wiring: three handlers in `App.tsx` (`handlePageSetupChange`/`handleTypographyChange`/`handleDisplayHeaderChange`) call the pure services and call `setActiveDocument` only when `ok && changed`.
- **Evidence (automated, 2026-10-16, final run after ALL code changes):**
  - `npx tsc --noEmit` → **exit 0**; `npm run build` → **exit 0**.
  - `npm run verify:p04-m1` → **12 passed, 0 failed**: (1) documented defaults; (2) five sizes + both orientations with agreeing geometry helpers; (3) margin presets write documented values, custom stays associated; (4) invalid margins/typography rejected with codes and no changes (incl. empty drafts); (5) heading scale derives from base size via the centralized tokens; (6) spacing/fallback updates commit without touching siblings; (7) updates immutable, unrelated document state preserved; (8) no-op patches return the same reference and create no dirty state; (9) serialize → deserialize round-trips all customized settings; (10) Reset Settings keeps content and metadata, restores defaults; (11) §8.4 display-header disable rule preserves the stored choice; (12) `duplicateDocument` carries every page/typography setting into the copy.
  - Regression, all exit 0: `verify:p02-m2` 11/11, `verify:p02-m3` 12/12, `verify:p02-m4` 9/9, `verify:p02-m5` 14/14, `verify:p03-m1` 14/14, `verify:p03-m2` 18/18, `verify:p03-m3` 18/18, `verify:p03-m4` 22/22, `verify:p03-m5` 17/17 → **135 prior checks + 12 new = 147, all green**; no existing test was weakened.

- **Scope completed:**
  - **Typed token contract** (`src/services/body-theme-tokens.ts`): `BodyThemeTokens` — a coherent, JSON-serializable contract every theme implements: `typography` (authoritative document family/fallbacks/base/line/paragraph spacing passed through unchanged, plus `headingSizesPt` derived by the P04-M1 centralized tokens); `headings` (per-level weight for hierarchy, alignment, margins, decoration); `paragraphs` (alignment, indent step); `lists` (indent, item spacing, marker — contract-only until list blocks exist); `tables` (border color/width, header background/border/text-color/weight, cell padding, divider color/width, bottom margin); `captions` (size, italic, margins, separate table/figure alignment, color); `separators` (section rule width/color, accent color — contract-only in P04-M2); `frame` (page framing style/color/width, limited to what @react-pdf can actually emit: `solid`/`dashed`/`dotted`); `pageFurniture` (size, offset, alignment, color); `colors` (text/heading/accent/rule/mutedText).
  - **Registry** (`src/services/body-theme-registry.ts`): `Record<BodyThemeId, BodyThemeDefinition>` — TypeScript rejects a missing entry, and `verify:p04-m2` re-checks completeness, id/key agreement, display-name uniqueness, non-empty intent/characteristics, and registry-color validity at runtime. Each entry carries stable metadata (display name, §8.3 design intent, planned structural characteristics) so P04-M3 inherits a catalogue instead of inventing one, plus a deep-frozen structural scaffold. Honest status: `classic-formal` = `default-verified`; the other nine = `foundation-planned` (scaffold only — the entry explicitly does not claim finished designs).
  - **Resolver**: `resolveBodyTheme(themeId, typography, monochrome)` — deterministic (identical inputs → deep-equal outputs), side-effect-free (fresh copies everywhere; never mutates inputs or the frozen registry), reuses `deriveHeadingSizesPt` (no second scale), returns `{ themeId, metadata, monochrome, tokens }`, and THROWS a listed-ids error for unknown theme ids instead of silently mapping to a finished theme.
  - **Monochrome policy**: `toMonochromeColor` strictly parses `#RGB`/`#RRGGBB`/`#RRGGBBAA`/`rgb()`/`rgba()`, converts by Rec.709 luminance to lowercase hex (alpha preserved as an 8th pair), round-trips grays unchanged (idempotent), and returns the documented `#000000` fallback for invalid input — never a malformed string. `applyMonochrome` transforms every color field while preserving all structural tokens (hierarchy, spacing, alignment, borders, widths, separators, frame style, furniture geometry, typography); it does not change the theme id, registry entry or any document content, and it is completely independent of the application `ThemeMode` (light/dark/multicolor).
  - **Renderable-path integration** (`src/renderer/poc/poc-document.tsx`, narrow — no rewrite): one `resolveBodyTheme` call site in `PocDocument`; the stylesheet was reduced to engine-level constants (PDF font mapping, flex/absolute geometry) so every themed value flows from the resolved tokens; headings/paragraphs/tables/captions/page furniture/ink/frame are all token-sourced; a shared caption helper serves table and figure captions. **Byte-identity protection:** novel properties (heading alignment/decoration, caption/table-header ink, page frame) are applied only when the theme deviates from the engine default, and classic-formal's tokens equal the exact pre-theming renderer constants — so the default path emits the same style set and the same PDF bytes as P04-M1/P02-M4.
- **Evidence (automated, 2026-10-17, final run after ALL code changes):**

  | `minimalist` | Regular-weight H4, widest 18/10 pt heading rhythm, left-ragged body at 16 pt, white table header capped by one decisive rule with a regular (non-bold) header row, dash markers, hairline `#dddddd` rules, quiet 8 pt furniture |

  | `framed-document` | The only page frame — solid 1.5 pt border on every sheet — with furniture inset 32 pt, 12/6 pt heading margins, bordered gray header + black header rule, italic captions |
  | `contemporary-asymmetrical` | Regular H4, left-ragged body with the deepest 24 pt indent steps, 20/10 pt heading rhythm, dash markers at 28 pt list indent, **right**-aligned table/figure captions and furniture |
  | `traditional-serif` | Book hierarchy — H2–H4 regular weight (BNNN), deepest 21 pt indent steps, hollow-circle markers at 26 pt, unfilled white header with regular-weight text and **no interior cell dividers** (borderless cells), 8.5 pt furniture at the lowest offset (34 pt) |

- **Scope completed:**
  - **Cover theme tokens + registry** (`src/services/cover-theme-tokens.ts`, `src/services/cover-theme-registry.ts`): the typed cover token contract (palette, typography, placement defaults, structure, decoration/ornaments, composition parameters) and exactly one frozen entry per authoritative `CoverThemeId` (typed `Record<CoverThemeId, …>` — compile-time exhaustive) carrying `displayName`, `intent`, `structuralDesign` and an honest `composition-verified` status. `resolveCoverTheme(themeId, { monochrome })` is deterministic and mutation-free, takes the monochrome flag explicitly (it throws if the options are malformed), and fails explicitly on unknown ids — no silent fallback theme.
  - **Ten finished §7.3 compositions in a fixed-wrapper first-page renderer** (`src/renderer/poc/poc-cover-page.tsx`): each theme is a distinct composition over shared components (principal bands, metadata column/centered/hero/control-table/framed variants, synopsis abstract, frame overlay, logo/background placement); `poc-document.tsx` mounts the cover as the FIRST page only when `frontPage.enabled`, with zero body furniture on it. Rendering correctness was proven by raster inspection and fixed at source: (a) band/root columns now grow without shrinking (`flexGrow: 1, flexShrink: 0`), so react-pdf's textkit can never ellipsize a Text allocated less height than its natural height; (b) metadata wrap rows carry 1 pt of slack (`metadataCellPt = (contentW − 1) / columns`) so cell widths that sum exactly to the container can never collapse to one column through 1-ulp float asymmetry; (c) `buildCoverCtx` takes page boxes from react-pdf's exact standard-size table (`PAGE_BOX_PT`: A4 595.28 × 841.89 etc., orientation-flipped) instead of mm-derived values; (d) corporate-band's fixed-height top/bottom bands became `minHeight` anchors (grow, never clip) with the gradient strip moved inside the band; (e) geometric's independently-anchored metadata/synopsis became one anchored foot stack with the floating image relocated to the open upper-right zone; (f) the technical grid sizes itself against the sidebar-offset column width it actually mounts in; (g) the cover renders inside a `fixed` absolute-positioned A4 wrapper — always exactly one cover page, true MediaBox, overflow clipped at the page edge.
  - **Pure front-page patch service + schema/persistence** (`src/services/front-page.ts`, `src/types/document.ts`, `src/types/defaults.ts`, `src/services/document-file.ts`): `FrontPagePatch` covers `enabled`/`themeId`/`monochrome`/`visibleFields`/`synopsis`/`logoWidthMm`/`logoAssetId`/`backgroundAssetId`/`frame`/`placement` (per-element align + band) with the module's established conventions — deterministic error codes, same-reference no-op on unchanged values (no false dirty), a real change stamping `modifiedAt` and touching only patched fields, synopsis trimmed with length enforcement, visibleFields validated against known + `custom:<id>` ids, frame/logo ranges enforced without silent coercion, and a placement whose both halves equal the theme default removing itself. `createDefaultFrontPage()` starts `enabled: true`, `classic-formal`, monochrome off, nine visible fields. `document-file.ts` validates the optional `frontPage.placement` only when present, so pre-P04-M4 schema-v1 files open unchanged with theme defaults (no version bump needed), and adds `io-read-image` for image pick/read failures.
  - **Image import pipeline** (`src/services/image-import.ts`, `src/services/image-io.ts`, `src-tauri/capabilities/default.json`): images are validated by magic number (PNG/JPEG/GIF/WebP honest messages — never trusted by extension), embedded into the document asset bundle as base64 records, and referenced by id; the native open dialog reads bytes through the added `fs:allow-read-file` capability; no external absolute path is ever persisted; cancelling the dialog is a true no-op; a failed import leaves the document untouched; **asset bytes are never overwritten** — a new import creates/replaces the asset record and removing a cover reference drops the reference while keeping bytes for other consumers (suite tests 15/16, shared-logo survival cases).
  - **Front Page panel + wiring** (`src/components/left-panel/FrontPagePanel.tsx/.css`, `LeftPanel.tsx`, `src/components/workspace/Workspace.tsx`, `src/App.tsx`, `src/components/preview-panel/PreviewPanel.tsx`): the Left Panel's "Front Page" section exposes the §7.1 controls — enable toggle, ten-option cover-theme `<select>` with registry display names and a live `intent` + `structuralDesign` description via `aria-describedby`, monochrome checkbox (document-only scope stated in the UI), synopsis textarea, visible-metadata checkboxes (incl. rename-safe `custom:<id>` entries and honest "selected but unavailable" handling), Title block placement selects with "Theme default (…)" reversion, logo/background Choose… + Remove buttons, logo width, and the §7.2 frame group (visible/style/thickness/radius/inset/colour) — all dispatched through `updateFrontPage` on the established immutable `ok && changed` path with inline `role="alert"` errors. `PreviewPanel` now passes the active document to `PocViewer`, so the PoC renders the real document including its cover; the §8.4 rule (display-header disabled-not-modified while the cover is enabled, stored setting retained) is enforced by the existing `pt-display-header` binding and verified live.
  - **Gallery, sample document and evidence artifacts** (`src/renderer/poc/poc-cover-gallery.tsx`, `cover-sample-doc.ts`, `cover-sample-assets.ts`, `fixtures/p04-m4/`): a ten-section colour gallery and a ten-section monochrome gallery rendered through the real registry/renderer path, plus a timestamp-free manifest (ids, names, per-artifact pages/bytes/sha256). Committed artifacts: `cover-gallery.pdf` **50,355 B** (10 pages), `cover-gallery-monochrome.pdf` **50,134 B** (10 pages), `cover-manifest.json` **10,897 B** — the suite renders and byte-compares them on every run (`UPDATE_ARTIFACTS=1` regenerates deliberately). - **Visual inspection (method and findings):** PDFs cannot be read directly by the agent, so each of the 20 gallery pages was rasterized in Node (`pdfjs-dist` 5.4.296 legacy build + `@napi-rs/canvas`, per-page PNGs) and inspected as an image. **All 20 pages PASS as correct, complete A4 covers.** Theme-by-theme: classic-formal (4-line title complete, 2-column pairs, complete synopsis), minimalist (quiet left stack, full inline metadata strip), corporate-band (bands grow instead of clipping, gradient strip inside the band, all 14 entries visible, last row not clipped), technical-grid (proper 3-column bordered grid, sidebar, heavy rule), editorial-split (panel holds all pairs + synopsis, right display title complete), photographic-hero (reversed title over the stage, 3×5 footer grid complete), institutional-frame (double frame, centred stack, bordered block), geometric (corner block, pill, floating image in clear zone, foot stack, all pairs with clean label/value gaps), scientific-publication (running head, abstract box, aligned key-value rows), administrative-record (running-head table, 2-column bordered table, abstract box); every monochrome page shows the identical composition with the palette converted to grayscale while assets stay untouched (the sample logo keeps its own colour — the recorded asset-bytes-never-overwritten decision). **Defects found by inspection and fixed at source before the final artifacts:** band compression ellipsizing titles/metadata (flex shrink → grow-without-shrink), 1-ulp wrap collapse doubling metadata height (1 pt cell slack), technical grid rendering 2 columns instead of 3 (sized against the wrong width), corporate bands clipping their rows (`minHeight` anchors), geometric's two independent foot anchors overlapping (single anchored stack), and finally a typographic jam where the fixed 62 pt metadata label column left no gap for full-width labels (6 pt guaranteed margin). After that last fix, SHA-256 comparison of the rasters proved **only page 8 of each gallery changed** — the other 18 PNGs byte-identical to the versions already inspected — and both changed pages were re-inspected. Coverage is airtight: every page of the final artifacts was viewed, with pages whose compositions were untouched by later cycles confirmed by hash or by the change-isolation of the cycle itself.

### 2026-10-18 — [MAJOR] P05-M1: Block Model and Insertion Framework

- **Phase:** Phase 05 — Core Modular Editor and Common Content Blocks
- **Status:** **VERIFIED** — the defined P05-M1 scope is implemented and passing on every executed check: `npx tsc --noEmit` + `npm run build` exit 0, the new `verify:p05-m1` 33/33, all thirteen prior suites 204 checks exit 0 (237 total), and a live agent-driven review of the real Windows/Tauri application 12/12 with 0 console errors/exceptions and 7 captured screenshots. Native Save/Open dialog interaction is recorded **NOT RUN** — the agent cannot drive native Win32 dialogs over CDP (P02-M2 environment precedent) and nothing was simulated; the persistence claim rests on the serializer/validator/fingerprint tests, and the native round-trip of a document containing inserted/reordered/respaced blocks is assigned to the `P05-GATE` owner walkthrough (assessment below). This is a checkpoint result only — the phase stays **IN PROGRESS**, no Phase 05 exit-criterion checkbox was flipped (criteria 3, 4, 5 and 6 gained P05-M1 evidence notes; checkboxes flip at `P05-GATE`), and `P05-M2` → `P05-GATE` are not started.
- **Branch:** `opencode/p05-m1-block-model-insertion-framework`, created from the pushed and verified `P04-GATE-R1` tip `721be3a` (not from `main`; `origin/main` untouched at `b56bc59`; working tree clean at branch creation). Commit title: `P05-M1: implement block model and insertion framework`.
- **What was implemented:**
  - **Typed block registry** (`src/services/block-registry.ts`): one `BlockRegistryEntry` per authoritative `BLOCK_TYPES` member (21/21) carrying the stable type id, display name, description, icon typed as `IconName` from the existing icon system (type-only import — zero React runtime coupling, Node-testable), an insertion-menu category (six ordered groups: Text, Callouts & Lists, Tables & Data, Media, Laboratory (ISO/IEC 17025), Page Layout) and a default factory returning a fresh schema-valid `ContentBlock` (envelope + type-specific data + valid default spacing ≥ 0 pt). `BLOCK_REGISTRY` is declared as `{ readonly [K in BlockType]: BlockRegistryEntry }` with `satisfies`, so a newly added union member / `BLOCK_TYPES` entry fails to compile until it receives a registry entry AND a default factory; runtime guards (`isBlockType`, `getBlockRegistryEntry` → `undefined`) turn cast or deserialized junk into explicit outcomes instead of silent breakage. Default factories reuse the twelve existing `types/template-defaults.ts` block factories where their signatures fit and add nine new ones (formula, image, horizontal-line, page-break, section-break, metadata-block, revision-history, reference, exhibit); `createBlock` checks every factory result against `entry.type` and throws on a violated invariant. `summarizeBlockData` is exhaustive over `AnyBlockData` with a `never` exhaustiveness check and drives the editor's truthful structural summaries (no content editor is implied for types whose editors arrive in `P05-M2`–`P05-M4`).
  - **Immutable block actions** (`src/services/block-actions.ts`): pure, React/DOM/Tauri-free transformations following the established `SettingsUpdateResult` conventions — success returns a fresh document plus `changed` and the operated `blockId`, real no-ops return the SAME document reference (no false Modified status), failures return `{ ok: false, code, message }` with understandable text and leave the document untouched. Operations: `createBlock` (throws `RangeError` for unregistered types), `insertBlock` at `start`/`end`/`at`/`before`/`after` (codes `unknown-block-type`, `missing-block`, `invalid-index`, `invalid-placement`), `duplicateBlock` (`structuredClone` deep copy — value-independent from the original — with a new stable envelope ID, inserted directly after the source), `reorderBlock` up/down (`at-boundary` at the ends; reference-preserving swap), `moveBlockTo` (absolute index for future drag reordering; same-reference no-op at the current index; `invalid-index`), `deleteBlock` (removes exactly one block; deleting the last block leaves a valid `blocks: []` document; `missing-block`), `updateBlockSpacing` (one pt field per call; finite and ≥ 0 only; negative/NaN/infinite/string/bad-field input rejected with distinct understandable messages; re-entering the current value is a same-reference no-op; only the one spacing field changes — block id, content, order and the other field are preserved). Every real change stamps `metadata.modifiedAt` (P02-M3 lifecycle policy) and never touches `createdAt`. `BlockOpOptions { now, genId }` injects the clock and the envelope-ID generator so `verify_p05_m1.mjs` runs deterministically (nested entity IDs always come from `newUUID()` and are never asserted by value). Selection is deliberately absent from this module: it is App session state, so selecting a block structurally cannot dirty or mutate the document.
  - **Stable-ID selection and App wiring** (`src/App.tsx`, `src/components/workspace/Workspace.tsx`): `selectedBlockId` lives in App state outside the document; a reconciliation effect clears it whenever the referenced block no longer exists (delete, clear, New/Open/Duplicate document replacement); insert and duplicate select the created block; deletion goes through the existing `ConfirmDialog` before the model change runs (modal captures the document it was opened against — no race); App handlers call the pure services and only `setActiveDocument` when `ok && changed`. Workspace forwards eight new typed props; the panel renders straight from `activeDocument.blocks` — there is no second block store anywhere.
  - **Functional editor panel** (`src/components/editor-panel/EditorPanel.tsx` + `.css`, replacing the Phase 01 placeholder): insertion card with a category-`optgroup` type menu covering all 21 types, an always-available **Add block** (append) button, **Insert after selected** disabled without a selection, an explicit selection hint, and a truthful note that rich content editing arrives in P05-M2–M4; the block list is keyed by `block.id` and each card shows the registry icon, type tag, `#n` position, structural summary, current pt spacing line, an `aria-pressed` select button (visibly and accessibly distinguished by accent border + SELECTED tag), ↑/↓ move buttons disabled at the boundaries, Duplicate, and a danger-styled Delete; the selected block expands an Above/Below pt input panel using the existing `parseNumberDraft` semantics (empty/non-numeric drafts become NaN and are rejected, never coerced) plus an `aria-live` notice carrying the service's message; a block-count badge sits in the header and an honest empty-state card guides first insertion when `blocks` is empty.
- **Schema/persistence compatibility:** no schema version bump; `ContentBlock`/`BlockSpacing` unchanged; `services/document-file.ts` validator untouched (its existing `blocks[]` checks already cover id / spacing-number / type-membership — the suite re-proves both positive and negative paths, and documents that file-level validation intentionally accepts any numeric spacing because negativity checks belong to input time in `block-actions`, so a future validator change flips suite test 31 deliberately); a document with ALL 21 block types inserted, spaced, duplicated and reordered round-trips `serializeDocument → deserializeDocument` deep-equal with identical fingerprints and byte-identical re-serialization, and a pre-existing schema-v1 document accepts new blocks without touching its older content (`verify:p05-m1` tests 29–33). Template flows are covered by the unchanged prior suites.
- **Automated verification (exact commands, 2026-10-18):** `npm run verify:p05-m1` → **33/33** (registry coverage/metadata/category partition; default-factory validity, type correctness and fresh unique IDs; guards + summaries; createBlock injection + RangeError; insertion at start/middle/end/before/after with reference-preserved neighbors, unknown-type/index/anchor rejections, all-21 insertion; duplicate independence under deep mutation; reorder/move with boundary/index/missing errors; delete incl. empty-document handling; spacing single-field/no-op/immutability/negative-NaN-Infinity-string-bad-field rejections; kitchen-sink serialize round-trip; validator positive/negative paths; Modified/Saved lifecycle + fingerprint stability; no selection keys inside the document). Then all thirteen prior suites re-run green — `verify:p02-m2` 11, `verify:p02-m3` 12, `verify:p02-m4` 9, `verify:p02-m5` 14, `verify:p03-m1` 14, `verify:p03-m2` 18, `verify:p03-m3` 18, `verify:p03-m4` 22, `verify:p03-m5` 17, `verify:p04-m1` 12, `verify:p04-m2` 14, `verify:p04-m3` 18, `verify:p04-m4` 25 = **204 checks, all exit 0 (237 total)**; `npx tsc --noEmit` → exit 0; `npm run build` → exit 0 (5.69 s; the pre-existing >500 kB chunk-size warning is unchanged). Suite bring-up fixes were script-side only (duplicating the wrong fixture block, comparing compact vs. pretty serializer output, reading the validator's per-field message from `detail`) — the implementation needed no change and no test assertion was weakened.
- **Live verification (real `npx tauri dev` Windows app, agent-driven via WebView2 CDP port 9223; driver `p05m1-live.mjs`, JSON report + 7 PNG screenshots in the approved temp location, not committed per repository policy):** **12/12 scenarios PASS** — boot (empty-state guidance visible, `0 blocks`, status `New`); four inserts through the real controls (order/type tags/summaries/position tags correct, boundary arrows disabled, status `Modified`); selection (visible selected state, exactly one `aria-pressed`, spacing panel bound to the heading's 8/4 pt defaults); Insert-after-selected (new block lands below the selection, becomes selected); Duplicate (copy with identical summary at the new position, selected); Reorder (paragraph moved up carries its content and its `#2` tag to the new position); Spacing (12 pt above commits; −1 pt rejected with an understandable notice and the stored value unchanged while the draft keeps the typed text for correction; 4 pt below commits and clears the notice); Delete (confirmation dialog appears — screenshot captured — exactly that block disappears and the selection clears); lab/data inserts (measurement `1 row × 2 columns`, exhibit `Exhibit A` truthful summaries); interface-theme switching multicolor → light → dark → back changes no document content or settings; deleting every block returns the empty-state guidance with `0 blocks` (status correctly stays `Modified`); console hygiene — 0 uncaught exceptions, 0 unexpected console errors (only the recorded pre-existing noise classes). Screenshots: `p05m1-live-01-boot.png`, `-02-blocks.png`, `-03-selected.png`, `-04-spacing.png`, `-05-confirm.png`, `-06-all-types.png`, `-07-empty-again.png`.
- **Evidence classes kept distinct:** (a) *automated* suite / `tsc` / build evidence — agent-executed; (b) *live* agent-driven CDP evidence in the real Tauri window — agent-executed DOM-level probes plus screenshots, not native-window interaction; (c) **NOT RUN — native Save/Open dialogs**: native Win32 dialogs cannot be driven over CDP and the agent did not simulate or claim them. **Assessment of limitation (c): it does not prevent P05-M1 from being VERIFIED** — no P05-M1 acceptance criterion requires the dialog interaction itself; persistence compatibility is fully evidenced at the serializer/validator/fingerprint layer (tests 29–33) plus the unchanged P02-M2/P02-M3 lifecycle suites; the save/open plumbing is byte-unchanged P02-M2 code that the owner has already verified end-to-end in the Phase 02/Phase 04 walkthroughs; and, following the Phase 04 precedent where gate-level native evidence was owner-provided, the native Save → New → Open round-trip of a document containing inserted/reordered/duplicated/respaced blocks is explicitly assigned to the `P05-GATE` owner walkthrough. If the gate judges dialog-level evidence mandatory for criterion 6 earlier than that, the same owner-procedure-then-closure pattern as `P03-GATE-R2`/`P04-GATE-R1` applies.
- **Renderer limitation recorded (exit criterion 5, not hidden):** the PoC renderer maps only heading/paragraph/table/image/list/horizontal-line/page-break/section-break (8 of 21); every other registered type renders in the preview as the visible italic marker `[P02-M4 PoC: block type "…" not yet mapped]` — inserted content is never silently dropped from the model or the preview. Per-type rendering arrives with `P05-M2`–`P05-M4`; painter/pagination fidelity remains Phase 08 scope.
- **Exit-criteria disposition:** no checkboxes flipped (they flip at `P05-GATE`). Criterion 3 (insertion/reorder/duplicate/delete) and criterion 4 (spacing at model/UI level) gained P05-M1 evidence notes; criterion 5 gained the renderer-marker recording note; criterion 6 gained the P05-M1 record note. Criteria 1 and 2 remain entirely for `P05-M2`–`P05-M4`.
- **Design decisions:** (1) the registry lives in `src/services/` beside the other registries and keeps every block data shape exclusively in `types/blocks.ts` — no parallel block model; (2) default factories reuse `template-defaults.ts` where signatures fit and otherwise carry light example content (`Section heading`, `New paragraph.`, …) so structural cards and the preview show where a block landed — editing/emptying that content is P05-M2+ scope; (3) duplication deep-copies by value with a new envelope ID while keeping nested entity IDs by value (identity within a block is scoped to that block; cross-document reminting remains `template-instantiation`'s `remintEntities`); (4) selection stays outside the document by design (structurally cannot dirty persisted content); (5) no validator change — spacing negativity/NaN checks belong to input time in `block-actions`, keeping the file format untouched and compatible.
- **Files created:** `src/services/block-registry.ts`, `src/services/block-actions.ts`, `verify_p05_m1.mjs`. **Files modified:** `src/components/editor-panel/EditorPanel.tsx` + `EditorPanel.css` (placeholder → functional panel), `src/components/workspace/Workspace.tsx` (pass-through props), `src/App.tsx` (selection state + reconcile effect + block handlers + delete confirmation), `package.json` (`verify:p05-m1` script), `project_roadmap.md` (this entry; summary rows; Phase 05 status; exit-criteria notes; checkpoint list; phase board row 05; minor checkpoint board). `project_details.md` NOT modified; `document-file.ts`, template services and the renderer untouched; no dependency, capability or fixture change.

No PR created, nothing merged, no force-push, `main` untouched.

### 2026-10-18 — [MAJOR] P05-M2: Headings and Rich-Text Paragraph

- **Phase:** Phase 05 — Core Modular Editor and Common Content Blocks
- **Status:** **VERIFIED** — the defined P05-M2 scope is implemented and passing on every executed check: `npx tsc --noEmit` + `npm run build` exit 0, the new `verify:p05-m2` 27/27, all fourteen prior suites 237 checks exit 0 (264 total; original recording of “231 / 258” was a summation error, corrected 2026-10-19 at P05-M3 — the per-suite counts in this entry sum to 237 prior / 264 total, re-confirmed by re-running all fourteen suites green), and renderer byte-identity re-proven (the committed P02-M4 PoC fixture renders byte-identical with the new code; `verify:p04-m3` 18/18 including its gallery-artifact and P02-M4 baseline byte-compares). Native Save/Open dialog interaction is recorded **NOT RUN** — the agent cannot drive native Win32 dialogs (P02-M2 environment precedent) and nothing was simulated; formatted-content persistence is evidenced by the serializer round-trip (`verify:p05-m2` test 24), and the native round-trip of a document containing numbered headings and formatted paragraphs is assigned to the `P05-GATE` owner walkthrough (assessment below). This is a checkpoint result only — the phase stays **IN PROGRESS**, no Phase 05 exit-criterion checkbox was flipped (criteria 1, 2 and 5 gained P05-M2 evidence notes; checkboxes flip at `P05-GATE`), and `P05-M3` → `P05-GATE` are not started.
- **Branch:** `opencode/p05-m2-headings-rich-text-paragraph` (the branch held only the untracked `src/services/heading-numbering.ts` at start; all P05-M2 work is committed on it; `main` untouched). Commit title: `P05-M2: implement headings and rich-text paragraph`.
- **What was implemented:**
  - **Heading-numbering engine** (`src/services/heading-numbering.ts`): `computeHeadingNumbering(blocks)` derives the display prefix for every `numbered: true` heading in document order, keyed by the stable envelope ID, and is the ONE engine shared by the editor panel and the renderer so both surfaces always agree. Documented deterministic rules: only numbered headings participate; a plain heading is skipped entirely (consumes no number, resets nothing); a level-L heading increments the level-L counter and resets deeper levels; counter segments still at zero are omitted (an H3 directly under an H1 with no H2 is "1.1", never "1.0.1"); level skipping is tolerated; the separator is two non-breaking spaces (`HEADING_PREFIX_SEPARATOR`). The prefix is DERIVED, never stored — `HeadingBlockData.text` remains the author's content verbatim, so repeated rendering or serialization can never accumulate duplicate numbering. Pure and deterministic (same ordered block list → same map; input never mutated — `verify:p05-m2` tests 1–4).
  - **Inline-span algebra and sanitizers** (`src/services/rich-text.ts`): pure, React/DOM-free functions over `InlineSpan[]` with character-range addressing — `spansToPlainText`/`plainTextToSpans` (plain-text projection the textarea edits), `normalizeHexColor` (`#RGB`/`#RRGGBB`/`#RRGGBBAA`, case-insensitive, optional `#`; named colours/`rgb()`/malformed/non-strings rejected as `null` — never coerced), `validateColorPatch` (clear semantics for `null`/`undefined`/`''`), `sanitizeHref` (https/http/mailto only, scheme compared case-insensitively and canonicalised; scheme-less bare hosts interpreted as `https://`; `javascript:`/`data:`/`vbscript:`/`file:`/control-character smuggling all rejected; empty clears), `normalizeSpans` (empty runs dropped, adjacent identically-styled runs merged, unsafe hrefs REMOVED and malformed colours dropped at normalisation time, false flags never stored), `applySpanFormatting` (bold/italic/underline/strikethrough set or removed over a `[start,end)` range with span splitting and re-merging; empty ranges and unknown flags rejected with deterministic codes), `applySpanLink` (sanitised set-or-clear over a range; an unsafe target is rejected BEFORE commit), `applySpanColor` (font colour and highlight over a range; invalid values rejected; `null` clears one property while preserving the other), `clearSpanFormatting` (removes every flag/colour/highlight/link in the range, text verbatim), `isFlagActiveOverRange`/`hrefOverRange` (toolbar pressed-state derived from the DATA, not transient editor state, per §10.2), and `remapSpansAfterTextEdit` (longest-common-prefix/suffix edit replay: untouched runs keep their formatting, the differing middle region is replaced, and inserted text is deliberately left unstyled so formatting never appears "by surprise" — applied afterwards via the toolbar; deterministic and pure).
  - **Immutable heading/paragraph content actions** (`src/services/block-content.ts`): extends the P05-M1 `block-actions` conventions to block CONTENT. `updateHeadingContent(doc, blockId, { level?, text?, numbered?, hAlign? })` — level must be 1–4 (`invalid-level`), text any string incl. empty (`invalid-text`), numbered a boolean (`invalid-numbered`), alignment left/center/right (`invalid-alignment`); `updateParagraphContent(doc, blockId, { spans?, hAlign?, indentLevel? })` — spans must be an array (`invalid-spans`) and are normalised through `normalizeSpans` on the way in (unsafe hrefs/malformed colours are sanitised at the MODEL boundary, not only in the UI), alignment includes justify (`invalid-alignment`), indent is an integer 0…8 (`invalid-indent`). Both: all validation BEFORE commit (a rejected patch leaves the document byte-identical), same-value patches are SAME-REFERENCE no-ops (no false Modified status, no `modifiedAt` churn), real changes stamp `metadata.modifiedAt` and change only the target block's data (ID, spacing, order and every other block preserved — the untouched blocks keep reference equality), and failures carry deterministic codes + understandable messages. `stamped`/`indexOfBlock` in `block-actions.ts` changed from module-private to exported (additive, no behavior change) for reuse.
  - **Heading content editor** (`src/components/editor-panel/HeadingContentEditor.tsx`): shown inside the selected heading block's card — H1–H4 level select (from the single authoritative `HEADING_LEVELS`), automatic-numbering checkbox, plain-text input, left/center/right alignment select, and a truthful derived-prefix preview ("Numbered as '1.2' in preview and export" / "Plain heading — no numbering prefix is printed") computed by the SAME engine the renderer uses (§10.1 editor/preview/export consistency). Every control commits through `updateHeadingContent`; inputs are controlled directly by the document model (no second copy of the heading state in component memory).
  - **Rich-text paragraph content editor** (`src/components/editor-panel/ParagraphContentEditor.tsx`): the textarea edits the PLAIN-TEXT projection of the authoritative spans; every keystroke replays onto the span model via `remapSpansAfterTextEdit` so inline formatting survives normal editing. Toolbar: B/I/U/S toggle buttons (pressed-state from `isFlagActiveOverRange` over the live selection), font-colour and highlight colour pickers with explicit clear buttons, a Clear-formatting button, a link row (URL input + Link/Unlink, unsafe targets surfaced via the existing `aria-live` notice before commit, current uniform selection link shown), alignment select (left/center/right/justify) and indent-level select 0–8. A live preview renders the stored spans with native elements. Paste is sanitised to PLAIN TEXT (control codes and line breaks removed — a paragraph is one text flow; separate lines are separate blocks). All algebra-level rejections surface inline without touching the document.
  - **Wiring** (`EditorPanel.tsx`/`.css`, `Workspace.tsx`, `App.tsx`): EditorPanel computes `headingPrefixes = computeHeadingNumbering(blocks)` once per block-list change (memoised) and renders the content editors inside the selected heading/paragraph card above the existing spacing panel; Workspace forwards two new typed props; App handlers call `updateHeadingContent`/`updateParagraphContent` against the authoritative `activeDocument` and only `setActiveDocument` when `ok && changed` — there is still exactly one block store. New CSS follows the existing variable/radius/transition conventions.
  - **Renderer support** (`src/renderer/poc/poc-document.tsx`, additive exports in `poc-node-entry.ts`): `PocDocument` computes `headingPrefixes` once and passes each heading its derived prefix; `Heading` prints `prefix + two-NBSP + text` for numbered headings (plain headings unchanged); `renderSpans` gained strikethrough (combined `'underline line-through'` when both apply), font colour, highlight background and safe links via react-pdf `Link` (href passed through `sanitizeHref` at render time — defence in depth). Every addition is deviation-only: spans without the new properties render exactly as before.
- **Schema/persistence compatibility:** no schema version bump; `HeadingBlockData`/`ParagraphBlockData`/`InlineSpan` unchanged; `document-file.ts` validator untouched; a document with numbered headings and a fully formatted paragraph (bold/italic/underline/strikethrough, colour, highlight, sanitised link, justify, indent) round-trips `serializeDocument → deserializeDocument` deep-equal and the numbering engine yields identical prefixes after the round-trip (`verify:p05-m2` test 24).

The PoC renderer still maps only 8 of 21 block types with the visible "not yet mapped" marker for the rest; lists/callouts/tables arrive with `P05-M3`–`P05-M4`.
- **Exit-criteria disposition:** no checkboxes flipped (they flip at `P05-GATE`). Criterion 1 (heading/paragraph usable) and criterion 2 (formatting survives serialization) gained P05-M2 evidence notes; criterion 5 gained the renderer-formatting + byte-identity + deferred-alignment note.
- **Design decisions:** (1) numbering is derived at display time from the authoritative block order and never persisted into `text` — a stored prefix could duplicate on renumbering and would fight the author's content; (2) the span algebra is a standalone pure module (`rich-text.ts`) consumed by the services, the UI and the renderer (via `sanitizeHref`/`normalizeHexColor`) — one implementation of every rule, Node-testable without React; (3) the paragraph editor uses a plain-text surface (textarea) with range-based toolbar actions rather than a contenteditable WYSIWYG — deterministic selection semantics, no browser-introduced markup, and the exact same algebra is unit-tested in Node; (4) inserted text is deliberately unstyled after an edit (never inherited-by-surprise) and formatting is an explicit toolbar action on a selection; (5) paste is plain-text only with control codes and line breaks removed — rich clipboard formats are discarded at the boundary; (6) model-boundary sanitisation (`normalizeSpans` inside `updateParagraphContent`) means even a future non-UI caller cannot store an unsafe link or malformed colour; (7) renderer links re-sanitise the href (defence in depth against documents authored before sanitisation existed); (8) block-alignment renderer precedence deferred with the byte-identity rationale above rather than silently regenerating verified artifacts.
- **Files created:** `src/services/heading-numbering.ts`, `src/services/rich-text.ts`, `src/services/block-content.ts`, `src/components/editor-panel/HeadingContentEditor.tsx`, `src/components/editor-panel/ParagraphContentEditor.tsx`, `verify_p05_m2.mjs`. **Files modified:** `src/services/block-actions.ts` (`stamped`/`indexOfBlock` exported — additive), `src/renderer/poc/poc-document.tsx` (numbering prefixes + span formatting + safe links in `renderSpans`/`Heading`/`BlockRenderer`/`PocDocument`), `src/renderer/poc/poc-node-entry.ts` (additive exports of the new services + numbering for the verify suite), `src/components/editor-panel/EditorPanel.tsx` + `EditorPanel.css` (content editors rendered in the selected card, memoised numbering, new styles), `src/components/workspace/Workspace.tsx` (pass-through props), `src/App.tsx` (heading/paragraph content handlers), `package.json` (`verify:p05-m2` script), `project_roadmap.md` (this entry; summary rows; Phase 05 status; exit-criteria notes; checkpoint list; phase board row 05; checkpoint board). `project_details.md` NOT modified; `document-file.ts`, template services, theme registries and the P04-M3/P02-M4 artifacts untouched; no dependency, capability or fixture change.

No PR created, nothing merged, no force-push, `main` untouched.

### 2026-10-19 — [MAJOR] P05-M3: Lists and Callouts

- **Phase:** Phase 05 — Core Modular Editor and Common Content Blocks
- **Status:** **VERIFIED** — the defined P05-M3 scope is implemented and passing on every executed check: `npx tsc --noEmit` + `npm run build` exit 0, the new `verify:p05-m3` 33/33, all fifteen prior suites 264 checks exit 0 (297 total), renderer byte-identity re-proven (the committed P02-M4 PoC fixture renders byte-identical; `verify:p04-m3` 18/18 including its gallery-artifact and P02-M4 baseline byte-compares), **and a live browser-driven walkthrough of the real app executed with screenshots** (list and callout editing end-to-end, PoC render of both blocks, dark-mode content invariance, 0 console errors/warnings). Native Save/Open dialog interaction is recorded **NOT RUN** — the agent cannot drive native Win32 dialogs (P02-M2 environment precedent) and nothing was simulated; list/callout persistence is evidenced by the serializer round-trip and legacy-fixture validity tests, and the native round-trip of a document containing nested lists and callouts is assigned to the `P05-GATE` owner walkthrough (assessment below). This is a checkpoint result only — the phase stays **IN PROGRESS**, no Phase 05 exit-criterion checkbox was flipped (criteria 1, 2 and 5 gained P05-M3 evidence notes; checkboxes flip at `P05-GATE`), and `P05-M4` → `P05-GATE` are not started.
- **Branch:** `opencode/p05-m3-lists-callouts` (branched from the `P05-M2` checkpoint commit; all P05-M3 work is committed on it; `main` untouched). Commit title: `P05-M3: implement lists and callouts`.
- **Scope implemented:**
  - **Pure list-numbering engine** (`src/services/list-numbering.ts`): `formatDecimal`, `formatAlpha` (bijective base-26 — 26 → z, 27 → aa), `formatRoman` (standard subtractive algorithm, `ROMAN_MAX` 3999 with decimal fallback beyond, non-positive → `0`), `formatListCounter` dispatching over the five `ListCounterStyle` values, and `computeListLabels(items, style, maxDepth)` — a single left-to-right pass maintaining per-depth counters that increment at an item's depth and reset deeper levels, omitting zero/not-begun segments; ancestor segments always render decimal and only the deepest segment takes the block's `numberStyle`, so `1.1.`-style nesting composes any per-level combination; `MAX_LIST_DEPTH` (8) mirrors the item-indent limit; labels are derived at render time and never stored anywhere in the document.
  - **Immutable list/callout content actions** (`src/services/list-callout-content.ts`): `updateListSettings` accepts any documented partial (`type`, `bulletStyle`, `numberStyle`, `markerSource`) with cross-field validation (`unknown-list-type` etc.), rejects identical-value writes as same-reference no-ops, and implements the type-switch policy — `checked` is inert when off-checklist but never deleted by a type switch, and switching **to** checklist ensures a boolean per item (absent → `false`); `updateListItemText` sanitises control characters and preserves span integrity; `addListItem` appends after a target item (or at the end) and returns the new stable ID so the editor can focus it; `removeListItem` removes exactly one item (last-item removal yields an empty-but-valid list); `moveListItem`/`moveListItemTo` are subtree-aware (an item plus its contiguous deeper followers move as one unit) and sibling-only (encountering an ancestor first rejects `at-boundary`), with explicit-index moves validating `invalid-index`; `indentListItem`/`outdentListItem` reject `at-depth-zero` (outdent at depth 0) and `depth-limit` (indent past `MAX_LIST_DEPTH`); `setItemChecked` is a same-value no-op otherwise; `applyListContentAction` dispatches the union with `id`-preserving failure results; `updateCalloutContent` validates `variant` against the known set (`unknown-callout-variant`), caps title at 120 chars (empty value deletes the field) and variant at 40, and sanitises the body text. Every action stamps `modifiedAt` only on a real change, targets by stable ID, and takes injected `now`/`genId` — the P05-M1/M2 conventions verbatim.
  - **Schema:** optional `ListBlockData.markerSource?: 'theme' | 'block'` added to `src/types/blocks.ts` — absent/`'theme'` is the pre-P05-M3 behaviour, so every existing document and fixture renders exactly as before; no schema version bump, validator untouched, legacy schema-v1 documents still validate (suite tests).
  - **Editors:** `ListContentEditor.tsx` (list-type select, marker-source select with a “Theme default” option, bullet-style or numbering-style select swapped by type, derived-numbering hint quoting the live labels, per-item text inputs that route through `remapSpansAfterTextEdit`, checklist checkboxes, per-item ↑/↓/indent/outdent/remove buttons whose `disabled` states reflect the true sibling/depth boundaries, Add item with focus management on the new input, Enter-in-item-to-add) and `CalloutContentEditor.tsx` (variant select that preserves unknown/custom values in its options, optional title with a Clear-title button, sanitised body textarea, structural preview mirroring the renderer's uppercase label + title + body). Wired App (`handleListContentAction` / `handleCalloutContentChange`) → Workspace → EditorPanel inside the selected-block card; insert-card hint updated to state that list and callout editing is available and only the table editor remains (`P05-M4`).
  - **Renderer** (`src/renderer/poc/poc-document.tsx`): `ListBlock` applies the marker-source policy (theme token unless `markerSource: 'block'`) and replaces static bullets with `computeListLabels` output for numbered lists while keeping theme bullets byte-identical for existing fixtures; arrow style renders as the WinAnsi-chevron `›`; checklist items render their checked marker; a new `CalloutBlock` (registered in the `BlockRenderer` switch) draws an uppercase semantic label (the meaning, never colour alone), an accent bar and a tinted bordered panel — hues info `#2f5d8a`, warning `#8a6400`, success `#2c6e49`, note `#4d4d4d`, danger `#9e2b25`; a custom variant falls back to neutral grey with the variant name uppercased as the label; monochrome mode routes every tint through the shared `toMonochromeColor`; an empty body omits the body node while label/title still render. `monochrome` is a new optional `BlockRenderer` prop (default `false` — additive), plumbed from `PocDocument`.
  - **Exports:** `poc-node-entry.ts` gained additive exports for `list-numbering` and `list-callout-content` so the verify script can drive them through the same compile path as the other suites.
- **Design decisions:** (1) **marker-source policy** — instead of making `bulletStyle` always win (which would change every theme-fixture byte), rendering honours `bulletStyle` only when `markerSource === 'block'`; absent/`'theme'` keeps the P04-M3 theme-token path. `numberStyle` is always honoured because body themes define no numbering token (numbered fixtures did not exist pre-P05-M3 — byte-safe by construction); suite test 28 proves all three states by byte-evidence (absent ≡ theme; block-disc differs on the dash theme; block-dash ≡ theme-dash). (2) **Arrow marker = WinAnsi chevron `›`** (U+203A, byte 0x9B) — standard-14 fonts cannot encode geometric triangles; the suite asserts the extracted `\u009b` bytes. (3) **Numbering composition** — ancestors always decimal, deepest segment uses `numberStyle` (upper-roman nesting yields `1.I.`), counters reset on shallower increments, markers never persisted. (4) **Movement/nesting** — subtree move, sibling-only boundaries, depth cap 8 with deterministic error codes (`at-boundary`, `at-depth-zero`, `depth-limit`, `invalid-index`). (5) **Checked policy** — inert off-checklist, preserved across type switches, ensured-on-switch-to-checklist, unrelated edits never touch it. (6) **Callout meaning is not colour-only** — uppercase label always present; monochrome reuses the shared converter; front-page monochrome remains independent of `pageTypography.monochrome` (P04-M4 design). (7) **`isListFailure` type predicate** exported from the service — the verify scripts' standalone `tsc` (without strict flags) does not narrow truthiness on the `ok` union (P05-M2 gotcha recurrence; script-side accommodation, no product change).
- **Automated verification (exact commands, 2026-10-19):** `npm run verify:p05-m3` → **33/33** (numbering: all five styles incl. bijective 27→aa, `ROMAN_MAX` fallback, per-depth counter resets, zero-segment omission, depth limit; settings: type/bullet/numbering/marker-source validation, checked-preservation across type switches, same-reference no-ops, `modifiedAt` policy; item text: sanitisation and span preservation; item management: add (incl. returned ID), remove (incl. last item), move/move-to/indent/outdent with every deterministic error code, subtree-carry behaviour, checklist state transitions, dispatcher coverage, unrelated-block reference integrity; callout: variant/title/body incl. limits, empty-title deletion and sanitisation; persistence: serialize → deserialize deep-equal round-trips for list and callout documents, legacy schema-v1 fixture validity, P05-M1/M2 spot-regressions; renderer: numbering styles and nesting labels, marker-source byte-evidence, arrow-chevron bytes, checklist byte-difference, callout labels and custom-variant fallback, monochrome `scn` grey extraction with `frontPage.enabled` disabled, no-dropped-text for every list/callout field, **P02-M4 PoC baseline byte-identical**).
